L01488 REJECTED TEXT="CPU time allocation: A process claims a fraction of processing capacity. Lottery scheduling allocates service probabilistically according to tickets. Stride scheduling accounts for each service event using a deterministic pass value, which reduces allocation variance. The design question is how a policy stays fair when tasks sleep, awaken, join, depart, or change weight." FIRST_USE=[] NUMBERS=[] GAP="no verified preceding-line derivation of this assertion"
L01489 NO_CLAIM TEXT="" FIRST_USE=[] NUMBERS=[] GAP="empty line"
L01490 REJECTED TEXT="Virtual-machine memory allocation: The host owns physical machine frames, but the guest chooses which guest pages are least useful. A host-only page eviction decision can harm the guest because the hypervisor lacks complete knowledge of its replacement policy. Ballooning transfers the reclamation decision to the guest by causing it to free its own pages. The design question is what happens if the host changes its request during balloon inflation, and what state the driver must bound or reset." FIRST_USE=["Virtual"] NUMBERS=[] GAP="no verified preceding-line derivation of this assertion"
L01491 NO_CLAIM TEXT="" FIRST_USE=[] NUMBERS=[] GAP="empty line"
L01492 REJECTED TEXT="Transparent page sharing: Two VMs can independently hold identical contents. Their different origins mean ordinary shared-file assumptions may not discover that duplication. Deduplication compares page contents and safely replaces mappings with a shared read-only backing page, with copying required upon writes. The design question is how to update all mappings and reference counts while preserving permissions and handling concurrent writes, migration, and invalidation." FIRST_USE=[] NUMBERS=[] GAP="no verified preceding-line derivation of this assertion"

L01494 REJECTED TEXT="Memory resource controls: An allocation policy must balance actual pressure, idle memory, memory access measurement, and guest isolation. If the hypervisor reclaims too early, paging overhead rises. If it waits too long, another VM cannot obtain needed frames. Waldspurger's ESX paper should be used to derive the policy, not to copy its historical numeric thresholds as modern kernel defaults."
